- **cis-tooling-evidence**: Record and validate exact CIS routing, generation, testing, run, and usage evidence for changed files. Use before handoff, verification, or pull-request readiness checks. ([andrewspiteri/CIS](https://github.com/andrewspiteri/CIS/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/cis-tooling-evidence))
- **cis-trans-gene-signature-annotation**: Use when significant cis/trans gene sets from iEDGE differential expression and mediation analysis need functional annotation, e.g. detecting hallmark pathways recurrently enriched across multiple SCNAs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cis-trans-gene-signature-annotation))
- **cis-trans-mediation-ranking**: Use when a cis/trans differential-expression signature table exists for SCNA events and candidate cis genes must be ranked by their trans mediation to designate the Rank-1 driver per alteration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cis-trans-mediation-ranking))
